> 69	### 2.1 Detailed Functional Requirements (com critério de aceite material)
> 70	
> 71	#### Fatia 0 — Pré-requisitos P0 (sem eles, tudo é falso-sucesso)
> 72	
> 73	**FR-CAD-015 — `whatsapp-webhook` alcançável + HMAC timing-safe + sink correto**
> 74	Adicionar o bloco `[functions.whatsapp-webhook] verify_jwt = false` em `supabase/config.toml` (hoje ausente ⇒ gateway responde 401 e o webhook nunca é chamado). Trocar a comparação de assinatura HMAC de `===` para `timingSafeEqual` (`_shared/oauth-state.ts`). Redirecionar o destino do payload de `whatsapp_leads` (tabela absorvida por OTD-MH-001) para `leads`/`lead_events`. Na variante BYOK (Fatia 4), resolver o tenant pelo `phone_number_id`/`waba_id` **antes** de buscar o app secret e validar (FM-CAD-09).
> 75	- **Critério material:** `curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" .../functions/v1/whatsapp-webhook` retorna **não-401** (challenge `hub.challenge` ecoado); `grep timingSafeEqual supabase/functions/whatsapp-webhook/index.ts` retorna ≥1 linha; **zero** referência a `===` na verificação de assinatura; um POST assinado com secret válido insere linha em `lead_events` (UUID retornado por `SELECT`).
> 76	
> 77	**FR-CAD-016 — `estimateNodeCost` case `publishSocial`**
> 78	Adicionar o `case 'publishSocial'` ao switch de `estimateNodeCost` (bug aberto: kind sem case ⇒ `undefined` em runtime; `strict:false` esconde). Corrigir **antes** de somar o kind `cadence` no `runAllCost`.
> 79	- **Critério material:** teste unitário prova que `runAllCost` de um grafo com nó `publishSocial` retorna número finito (não `NaN`); checklist manual de 6 edições de canvas registrado no PR (FM-CAD-16).
> 80	
> 81	**FR-CAD-017 — `erase_lead()` cancela agendamentos futuros em cascata**
> 82	Estender `erase_lead()` (`…20260603000000…:130-169`) para cancelar, **na mesma transação**, todos os agendamentos futuros do lead: linhas pendentes em `scheduled_posts` + fila de DM (`cadence_dispatches` com `status` não-terminal). Sem isso o direito de eliminação (LGPD Art. 16/18 VI) é ficção (G9).
> 83	- **Critério material:** após `SELECT erase_lead('<uuid>')`, `SELECT count(*) FROM scheduled_posts WHERE ... AND status='pending'` = **0** e `SELECT count(*) FROM cadence_dispatches WHERE ... AND status IN ('queued','publishing')` = **0** para aquele lead; a operação é atômica (rollback comprovado se qualquer passo falhar).
> 84	
> 85	**FR-CAD-018 — `auto-publish` em pg_cron + `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`**
> 86	Migrar `auto-publish` de crontab de SO de um único host para **pg_cron**, drenando `scheduled_posts` com `SELECT … FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`. Pré-requisito declarado ("antes de ligar recorrência") — a cadência não pode nascer sobre um sink sem lock num único host de SO.
> 87	- **Critério material:** `SELECT jobname, schedule FROM cron.job WHERE command ILIKE '%auto-publish%'` retorna ≥1 linha; a query de claim contém `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` (grep na migration); smoke prova que dois runs concorrentes **não** publicam a mesma linha 2× (FM-CAD-01).
> 88	
> 89	#### Fatia 1 — Cadência de Publicação (ZERO app review)
> 90	
> 91	**FR-CAD-001 — Nó `cadence` no Canvas Studio**
> 92	Registrar `kind: 'cadence'` no canvas registry, categoria **Publish**. `CadenceData = { kind:'cadence', label, status, sourceAssetId?: string (creative_assets.id owner-scoped), planId?: string, program: CadenceStep[], enabled: boolean, output?, error? }`. `resolveExecutePayload → null`; `estimateNodeCost → 0` (não viola G7 — a cobrança é do **ciclo**, não do nó). Excluído do gate "runnable" de `useCanvasPipeline.ts:33-50` (não exige `prompt`). Único `<Handle>` target `input_asset` (id inline, molde `PublishSocialNode`). Aliases pt-BR no registry: **cadência / agendar / recorrente / cron / calendário**.
> 93	- **Critério material:** as **6 edições** (types/registry/node/nodeTypes/RightPanel/inspector) presentes no diff; adicionar o nó ao grafo persiste em `vm_canvas_projects.graph` (SELECT do jsonb mostra `"kind":"cadence"`); `runAllCost` do grafo com o nó `cadence` **não** vira NaN e soma 0 para o nó.
> 94	
> 95	**FR-CAD-002 — Estender `autopilot_plans` (migration única)**
> 96	`ALTER TABLE autopilot_plans`: `DROP CONSTRAINT <platforms_check>`; `ADD COLUMN plan_kind text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'viral' CHECK (plan_kind IN ('viral','cadence'))`; `ADD COLUMN recurrence jsonb` (`{frequency, days, hours, minutes, tz}`); `quiet_hours jsonb`; `overlap_policy text DEFAULT 'skip'`; `catchup_window interval DEFAULT '6 hours'`; `jitter_seconds int DEFAULT 0`; `program jsonb` (molde `sequences.steps`+`current_step`); `channel_allowlist text[]` (por plano, não constante); `budget_cap_mco int NOT NULL` (em mcoCoins, nunca USD). RLS owner-scoped **preservada**; nenhuma tabela paralela, nenhum 3º driver (OTD-CAD-003).
> 97	- **Critério material:** `\d autopilot_plans` (ou `information_schema.columns`) lista as 9 colunas novas com os defaults/CHECK exatos; a constraint `platforms_check` **não** existe mais; `/security-review` da migration retorna **NO FINDINGS**; planos `plan_kind='viral'` existentes continuam válidos (regressão smoke do autopilot verde).
> 98	
> 99	**FR-CAD-003 — Ledger `cadence_dispatches`**
> 100	Tabela nova: `plan_id`, `step_index`, `occurrence_at`, `idempotency_key`, `channel`, `status`, `target_ref`, `error`, `external_usd_cost numeric`, `cost_source text`. Índice **único PARCIAL com predicado declarado**: `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX cadence_dispatches_idem ON cadence_dispatches (idempotency_key) WHERE status <> 'failed';` — é o predicado (e só ele) que produz "**falha libera** a chave / **sucesso prende**". RLS: `select_own` (cliente lê) + `insert` service-role + `update` service-role (`status`/`error`/`external_usd_cost`). **O cliente nunca escreve.** Não é append-only puro (semântica transiciona; se o Sovereign preferir append-only, cai para at-most-once — OTD-CAD-006).
> 101	- **Critério material:** o índice aparece em `pg_indexes` com o predicado `WHERE (status <> 'failed'::text)`; um segundo `INSERT` com a mesma `idempotency_key` e `status<>'failed'` viola a unique (SQLSTATE 23505 ou `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` retorna 0 linhas); um `INSERT` do cliente (anon/user role) é **negado** por RLS; `/security-review` NO FINDINGS.
> 102	
> 103	**FR-CAD-004 — Roteamento do tick por `plan_kind`**
> 104	O tick vivo `autopilot-cadence-cron` (*/15, gate `Authorization !== Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY` → 403, `user_id` **server-trusted da linha, nunca do body** — SOP `autopilot-cron-identity.md`) passa a **rotear por `plan_kind`**: `viral` → `autopilot-run` (como hoje); `cadence` → `cadence-run` (edge nova). **Nenhum job pg_cron novo** (o mesmo tick varre as duas classes; Supabase recomenda ≤~8 jobs concorrentes). pg_cron roda em **GMT** ⇒ preferência de horário **jamais** vira cron expression.
> 105	- **Critério material:** `SELECT count(*) FROM cron.job` **inalterado** antes/depois (nenhum job adicionado); log do tick mostra dispatch a `cadence-run` para linhas `plan_kind='cadence'` e a `autopilot-run` para `plan_kind='viral'`; um POST ao tick sem `Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY` retorna **403**.
> 106	
> 107	**FR-CAD-005 — Edge `cadence-plan` (arm)**
> 108	Edge fn nova `cadence-plan`: recebe o payload do inspector, faz **upsert** do plano em `autopilot_plans` (`plan_kind='cadence'`) e arma `next_run_at` computado em **UTC** a partir de `recurrence`. Gate `Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY`→403 quando cron; JWT de usuário quando UI (verify_jwt=false → **ES256 JWKS verify**, nunca `atob` cego). `user_id` **server-trusted**. Criar o plano custa **0 mco**. Inspector faz poll das últimas N dispatches + `next_run_at` no **molde `useVoiceRenderPoll`** (id no `data` do nó, sobrevive a refresh — nunca `sleep(8s)×25`).
> 109	- **Critério material:** POST válido retorna `plan_id` (UUID) e o `SELECT` da linha mostra `plan_kind='cadence'` + `next_run_at` em UTC coerente com a `recurrence`; JWT forjado/ausente → 401/403; o poll re-hidrata após refresh do browser (id persistido no grafo); **0** débito de mco na criação (`profiles.mco_balance` inalterado).
> 110	
> 111	**FR-CAD-006 — Edge `cadence-run` (drain + gate chain + dispatch + re-arm)**
> 112	Edge fn nova invocada pelo tick para `plan_kind='cadence'`. Sequência: (1) **drain** dos planos vencidos com `SELECT … FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`; (2) resolve o **subject** owner-scoped (`creative_assets.id`); (3) **gate chain** server-side (FR-CAD-008/009/010 + opt-out + jurisdição — nunca LLM); (4) **dispatch**: enfileira em `scheduled_posts` via o contrato `metadata.reshape` + `schedule:true` + `publish_at` ISO (Amendment 22) — o nó **enfileira, não publica**; (5) **ledger** `cadence_dispatches` via `INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING RETURNING` sob o índice parcial; (6) **re-arm** `next_run_at` (FR-CAD-007) ou `deactivate`. `overlap_policy=skip` (ciclo anterior rodando ⇒ pula). Reconciliação: `sent` **só do webhook de status**, nunca do 202 (FM-CAD-05). Se houver passo `generate`, cobrança via `begin`/`finalize` atômico (G7 = invariante do ciclo).
> 113	- **Critério material:** um plano com `next_run_at` vencido produz **1** linha em `cadence_dispatches` + **1** linha `pending` em `scheduled_posts` (UUIDs); tick sobreposto/retry **não** cria 2º dispatch para a mesma `(plan_id, step_index, occurrence_at)` (smoke re-executável, 0 double-post); nenhuma linha marca `sent` a partir de um 202 (só do webhook de status); `next_run_at` avança em UTC.
> 114	
> 115	**FR-CAD-007 — Re-arm UTC generalizado**
> 116	Generalizar o re-arm de `autopilot-run/index.ts:310-314` (`now + interval_days`) para um calculador UTC de `{frequency, days, hours, minutes, tz}`. O cron **nunca** carrega preferência de horário — o horário do sujeito é convertido para UTC e gravado em `next_run_at`. Ressuscita `profiles.timezone` como SSOT de fuso (cascata `recurrence.tz → profiles.timezone → America/Sao_Paulo`).
> 117	- **Critério material:** dado `recurrence {frequency:'weekly', days:[1,3,5], hours:9, minutes:0, tz:'America/Sao_Paulo'}`, o `next_run_at` gravado corresponde à próxima 2ª/4ª/6ª 09:00 BRT **convertida para UTC** (12:00Z fora do DST); teste unitário do calculador cobre GMT vs. fuso do usuário (FM-CAD-06); `profiles.timezone` passa a ter ≥1 leitor (grep prova).
> 118	
> 119	**FR-CAD-008 — Quiet-hours + frequency-cap HALT**
> 120	Quiet-hours: janela `[start,end]` + weekdays no **fuso do sujeito** (cascata acima); dispatch em quiet-hours é **suprimido**. Frequency-cap: chave `(user_id, channel)` + janela + threshold, com **HALT** (não reenfileiramento — semântica Knock). Teto autoritativo = `channel_profiles.cadence` (`target_per`/`count_min`/`count_max`), que ganha o **primeiro leitor** do repo (OTD-CAD-008 = autoritativo). Decisão pendente: cap por `(user,channel)` vs `(user,channel,conta)` (OTD-CAD-017 — a selar na SDD). Contador de janela **não existe hoje** (a SDD deve criá-lo).
> 121	- **Critério material:** dispatch cujo horário cai em quiet-hours no fuso do sujeito **não** produz linha em `scheduled_posts`; dispatch cujo canal já atingiu o teto de `channel_profiles.cadence` na janela faz **HALT** (0 publicação, toast pt-BR informativo, **não** reenfileira); `channel_profiles.cadence` tem ≥1 leitor (grep prova).
> 122	
> 123	**FR-CAD-009 — Dedup/digest**
> 124	`digestKey = (user, channel, dia)` colapsa N vencimentos do mesmo canal no mesmo dia em **1** publicação (ou 1 carrossel — carrossel gated por OTD-CAD-005). É a diferença estrutural entre **cadência** e **flood**.
> 125	- **Critério material:** dado um plano que produz 3 vencimentos do mesmo canal no mesmo dia, o dispatch resulta em **1** linha em `scheduled_posts` (não 3); smoke prova o colapso.
> 126	
> 127	**FR-CAD-010 — A/B determinístico**
> 128	Fórmula **exata** (a SDD copia literalmente): `mod(abs(hashtext(subject_ref || ':' || experiment_key)::bigint), 100) < ratio`. **Proibido** `hashtext(...) % 100 < ratio` (retorna `integer` com sinal; `-42 % 100 = -42 < ratio` para qualquer ratio ≥ 0 ⇒ ~50% caem no bucket A, enviesando em silêncio). O cast para `bigint` vem **antes** do `abs` (`abs(int4min)` estoura). Estável entre retries (nunca `random()`). Loop fecha em `creative_metrics.hook_rate` (OTD-CAD-016 — a ingestão confiável é pré-requisito de **ligar** o A/B).
> 129	- **Critério material:** teste unitário prova que, com `ratio=30`, ~30% (±tolerância) de N subjects caem no bucket A e o mesmo subject cai **sempre** no mesmo bucket entre retries; teste negativo prova que a variante `% 100` produziria ~50% (regressão barrada).
> 130	
> 131	**FR-CAD-011 — Custo projetado por ciclo + `budget_cap_mco`**
> 132	O inspector **DEVE exibir** o custo projetado por ciclo em mco = `Σ (custo em mco de cada canal do plano) × ocorrências até o cap`, em `--gold`, **antes** do botão Armar. Ex.: plano diário 30d com X-post-com-link (45 mco) projeta **1.350 mco**. `budget_cap_mco` é **NOT NULL** e em **mcoCoins** (nunca USD). Obrigação compensatória do quote=0: "não cobrar ≠ não medir". Custo externo BYOK que o tenant absorve é **registrado** em `external_usd_cost` + `cost_source` **no reconcile** (webhook de status, nunca no 202), e agregado no `collective_efficiency_ledger`.
> 133	- **Critério material:** o inspector renderiza o número em mco antes de armar (screenshot Vision QA); tentar armar sem `budget_cap_mco` → **422** (NOT NULL); um dispatch BYOK com custo externo grava `external_usd_cost` numérico + `cost_source` na linha e a cobrança em mco é **0** (`SELECT` prova ambos); o custo entra no `collective_efficiency_ledger`.
> 134	
> 135	#### Fatia 3 — Canais desimpedidos BYOK
> 136	
> 137	**FR-CAD-014 — Telegram + Email Resend (BYOK per-user, fail-closed)**
> 138	**Telegram:** `telegram_bot_token` no pool per-user (coluna cifrada + UI Settings). Fail-closed `402 telegram_not_configured` (estruturado, com `action` de configuração) quando ausente. Opt-in **estrutural** via `/start` — o bot não fala com quem nunca deu `/start`. Custo Meta-free (US$0). **Email (Resend):** `resend_api_key` per-user cifrada + **domínio verificado do tenant**; flip do `nurture-dispatch` de `delivery:'gated'` → `'sent'`. O **rodapé (endereço postal + identificação de anúncio + opt-out) e o disclosure de IA são montados server-side** e concatenados ao corpo — **jamais** delegados ao prompt do LLM (G10/G12; FM-CAD-12). Flip só com GO Sovereign + domínio verificado.
> 139	- **Critério material:** Telegram sem token configurado → resposta **402** com body `{ error:'telegram_not_configured', action:... }`; envio a chat que nunca deu `/start` é bloqueado; Email — o corpo enviado contém o rodapé/opt-out/disclosure **concatenado server-side** (não presente no prompt, prova por diff do payload); flip `gated→sent` só ocorre com a flag de GO e domínio verificado (`SELECT` do estado).
> 140	
> 141	#### Fatia 2 — Laço Inbound IG `[PROBE-GATED]`
> 142	
> 143	> ⚠️ **FR-CAD-012/013 são especificação CONGELADA.** Não geram código antes do **probe FM-CAD-02** fechar: DM real de **terceiro sem role no app** aparecendo no banco + **payload cru** de `entry.messaging` arquivado (FM-CAD-13). Se o DM não chegar sob Standard Access, **App Review vira pré-requisito** e a Fatia 2 inteira está bloqueada. Nascem `delivery:'gated'`; flip para `'sent'` só com GO Sovereign.
> 144	
> 145	**FR-CAD-012 `[PROBE-GATED]` — Ingest inbound IG**
> 146	Estender `instagram-webhook` (reusando o esqueleto: HMAC `timingSafeEqual` + `hub.challenge`) para tratar `entry.messaging` (DM) e `entry.changes`+`comments`. Sink muda de `infra_health_logs` para: `lead_events(event_type='message_received')` + `leads` + `notify()` → `social_threads`/`social_messages`. `social_threads`/`social_messages` são **obrigatórias** (a Conversations API só devolve as **20 msgs mais recentes** ⇒ a Meta não é banco de histórico). Relógios derivados **no servidor**: `csw_expires_at` (24h) do inbound + `private_reply_deadline` (7d) por comentário.
> 147	- **Critério material:** o **probe** precede o código — a linha `lead_events(message_received)` de um DM real de terceiro sem role aparece por `SELECT` (UUID) e o payload cru fica arquivado; só então o branch `entry.messaging` é codado com o shape **fixado** pelo payload (nunca às cegas — FM-CAD-13); histórico persiste em `social_threads` (não depende das 20 da Meta).
> 148	
> 149	**FR-CAD-013 `[PROBE-GATED]` — Resposta na janela legal**
> 150	Gate chain server-side de resposta (nunca LLM): (a) **private reply** comment→DM 1× por comentário — `UNIQUE(comment_id)` na fila (retry duplicado = violação de política, não bug de UX — G2), CHECK de idade **no send**, deadline 7d; (b) **resposta in-window** dentro de 24h da CSW. Fora da janela: **nada promocional** (G6, classificador in/out-window no servidor). Cold DM **impossível de representar**: a fila tem **FK NOT NULL** para um inbound do mesmo `user_id` — sem inbound, a linha não existe (G1/FM-CAD-03). A **Human Agent tag** é **fisicamente inalcançável** do caminho service-role/pg_cron (separação de caminho, não `if` — G5). IG/Messenger no app **global** com cap de fan-out por-APP + kill-switch global + telemetria de feedback negativo por-app (OTD-CAD-018). Nasce `delivery:'gated'`. Resposta com link afiliado + UTM → `creative_metrics` → `collective_efficiency_ledger`.
> 151	- **Critério material:** tentar enfileirar DM sem inbound correspondente → **viola FK** (linha não existe); 2º private reply para o mesmo `comment_id` → viola `UNIQUE` (23505); dispatch fora da janela de 24h é **bloqueado no send** pelo classificador server-side; a Human Agent tag não é setável por nenhum caminho service-role (prova de separação); todo dispatch nasce `delivery:'gated'` (flip só com GO Sovereign).
> 152

> 155	## 3. Non-Functional Requirements
> 156	
> 157	| ID | Category | Requirement | Target | Measurement |
> 158	|----|----------|-------------|--------|-------------|
> 159	| NFR-CAD-001 | Correctness (idempotência) | Tick sobreposto/retry não produz double-post | **0** dispatches duplicados por `(plan_id, step_index, occurrence_at)` | Smoke re-executável + `count(*)` no ledger |
> 160	| NFR-CAD-002 | Correctness (materialidade) | `sent` só a partir do webhook de status, nunca do 202 | 100% | Auditoria de reconcile (FM-CAD-05) |
> 161	| NFR-CAD-003 | Correctness (fuso) | `next_run_at` computado em UTC; cron nunca carrega horário | 100% dos re-arms | Teste unitário do calculador (FM-CAD-06) |
> 162	| NFR-CAD-004 | Security | Edge fns verify_jwt=false → **ES256 JWKS verify** (nunca `atob` cego); HMAC `timingSafeEqual` | 100% dos endpoints | `/security-review` + teste ES256 |
> 163	| NFR-CAD-005 | Security (tenancy) | Credencial de provider **BYOK per-user** (`auth.uid()`); env = só infra; fail-closed 402/501 estruturado | 100%; **0** `Deno.env.get(<provider>)` em fluxo user-facing | `/security-review` + smoke fail-closed |
> 164	| NFR-CAD-006 | Security (RLS) | RLS default-deny owner-scoped; `cadence_dispatches` cliente **nunca** escreve | 100% | `/security-review` NO FINDINGS |
> 165	| NFR-CAD-007 | Economics | Cobrança **só via RPC atômica** `begin`/`finalize` (nunca client-side); quote==charge invariante do **ciclo** | 100% | Auditoria do ledger (G7) |
> 166	| NFR-CAD-008 | Economics (visibilidade) | Custo externo BYOK **registrado** em `external_usd_cost` mesmo com mco=0 | 100% dos dispatches BYOK | `SELECT` do ledger + `collective_efficiency_ledger` |
> 167	| NFR-CAD-009 | Resilience | Backlog storm após queda ≤ `catchup_window` (6h) não floda; fan-out bounded | `MAX_PER_RUN=50` / `CONCURRENCY=6` | Load smoke (FM-CAD-07) |
> 168	| NFR-CAD-010 | Compliance (estrutural) | Guardrails G1–G14 impostos por **construção** (FK/UNIQUE/fail-closed/server-side), nunca via LLM | 14/14 | `/security-review` + smoke IoC (G13) |
> 169	| NFR-CAD-011 | Compliance (LGPD) | `erase_lead()` cancela agendamentos futuros na mesma transação | 100% atômico | AT-CAD-017 |
> 170	| NFR-CAD-012 | Infra (contenção) | Drain via `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`; **nenhum** job pg_cron novo | +0 jobs cron | `count(*) FROM cron.job` invariante |
> 171	| NFR-CAD-013 | Performance | Edge `cadence-run` p95 por tick (fan-out bounded) | < 5s por batch de 50 | Supabase logs |
> 172	| NFR-CAD-014 | UX | Inspector poll sobrevive a refresh (molde `useVoiceRenderPoll`); nunca `sleep(8s)×25` | 100% | Vision QA + code review |
> 173	| NFR-CAD-015 | Observabilidade | Toda falha → `infra_health_logs` (`service='spaces-cadence'`) + nó de observação na Knowledge Mesh por ciclo | 100% dos paths de erro | Query de telemetria |

> 176	
> 177	## 4. Interface Requirements
> 178	
> 179	### 4.1 UI → Hook → Edge → DB Contract
> 180	
> 181	```
> 182	[Component: CadenceNode + CadenceInspector (RightPanel)]
> 183	  ↓ arma o plano / faz poll (molde useVoiceRenderPoll)
> 184	[Hook: useCanvasStudio → cadence-plan invoke]
> 185	  ↓ upsert plano + arm next_run_at UTC
> 186	[Edge Function: cadence-plan]  (verify_jwt=false → ES256 JWKS)
> 187	  ↓ escreve
> 188	[Table: autopilot_plans (plan_kind='cadence')]
> 189	
> 190	[Cron: pg_cron */15 → autopilot-cadence-cron]  (gate Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY → 403)
> 191	  ↓ roteia por plan_kind
> 192	[Edge Function: cadence-run]  (drain FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED)
> 193	  ↓ gate chain server-side → dispatch
> 194	[Table: scheduled_posts (metadata.reshape + schedule:true + publish_at)]  ← SINK one-shot
> 195	  ↓ ledger + re-arm
> 196	[Table: cadence_dispatches (idempotency índice parcial)]
> 197	  ↓ auto-publish (pg_cron + SKIP LOCKED) DRENA e publica
> 198	[reconcile webhook de status] → external_usd_cost + cost_source → collective_efficiency_ledger
> 199	```
> 200	
> 201	### 4.2 External API Contracts
> 202	
> 203	#### `POST /functions/v1/cadence-plan`
> 204	**Auth:** Bearer JWT (user scope) → **ES256 JWKS verify** (nunca `atob`). `user_id` server-trusted.
> 205	**Request:**
> 206	```typescript
> 207	{
> 208	  sourceAssetId: string,           // creative_assets.id (owner-scoped)
> 209	  recurrence: {                    // computado em UTC no servidor
> 210	    frequency: 'daily'|'weekly'|'monthly',
> 211	    days: number[],                // weekdays (1=Mon) ou dias do mês
> 212	    hours: number, minutes: number,// múltiplos de 5 (Knock)
> 213	    tz: string                     // IANA; cascata → profiles.timezone → America/Sao_Paulo
> 214	  },
> 215	  quiet_hours?: { start: string, end: string, days: number[] },
> 216	  channel_allowlist: string[],     // por plano (auto-publish MENOS X na Fatia 1)
> 217	  budget_cap_mco: number,          // NOT NULL, em mcoCoins
> 218	  overlap_policy?: 'skip',
> 219	  catchup_window?: string,         // interval, default '6 hours'
> 220	  jitter_seconds?: number,
> 221	  program?: CadenceStep[]          // molde sequences.steps + current_step
> 222	}
> 223	```
> 224	**Response (200):**
> 225	```typescript
> 226	{ plan_id: string, next_run_at: string /* ISO UTC */, projected_cost_mco: number }
> 227	```
> 228	**Error codes:**
> 229	| Code | Condition |
> 230	|------|-----------|
> 231	| 401 | JWT ausente/inválido (falha ES256) |
> 232	| 402 | `<provider>_not_configured` (BYOK fail-closed) — body `{ error, action }` |
> 233	| 403 | Cron sem `Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY` |
> 234	| 422 | Validação (ex.: `budget_cap_mco` ausente; `recurrence` inválida) — campo + msg pt-BR no body |
> 235	| 500 | Erro interno (logado em `infra_health_logs`) |
> 236	
> 237	#### `POST /functions/v1/cadence-run` (invocada pelo tick, não pela UI)
> 238	**Auth:** `Authorization === Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY` (senão **403**). `user_id` **server-trusted da linha** (nunca do body).
> 239	**Behavior:** drain `SKIP LOCKED` → resolve subject owner-scoped → gate chain → dispatch a `scheduled_posts` → `INSERT cadence_dispatches ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING RETURNING` → re-arm `next_run_at` | deactivate. Reconciliação `sent` só do webhook de status.
> 240	
> 241	---
> 242	
> 243	## 5. Data Requirements
> 244	
> 245	> Entidades: **NOVA** `cadence_dispatches`; **NOVA** `social_threads`/`social_messages` (Fatia 2); **REUSA-ESTENDE** `autopilot_plans`; **REUSA** `scheduled_posts` (sink), `lead_events`/`leads`/`marketing_consents`, `sequences`/`sequence_enrollments` (molde), `profiles.timezone` + `channel_profiles.cadence` (ressuscitados), RPCs `begin`/`finalize`.
> 246	
> 247	| Field (tabela) | Type | Nullable | Validation Rule | Error Message (PT-BR) |
> 248	|----------------|------|----------|-----------------|----------------------|
> 249	| `autopilot_plans.plan_kind` | text | No | `IN ('viral','cadence')` DEFAULT `'viral'` | "Tipo de plano inválido." |
> 250	| `autopilot_plans.recurrence` | jsonb | Yes (No p/ cadence) | `{frequency,days,hours,minutes,tz}`; `minutes % 5 = 0` | "Recorrência inválida (minutos devem ser múltiplos de 5)." |
> 251	| `autopilot_plans.budget_cap_mco` | int | **No** | `> 0`, em mcoCoins | "Defina o teto de custo por ciclo (em mcoCoins)." |
> 252	| `autopilot_plans.channel_allowlist` | text[] | Yes | ⊆ allowlist de `auto-publish`; **X fora** na Fatia 1 | "Canal não permitido nesta fatia." |
> 253	| `autopilot_plans.overlap_policy` | text | Yes | DEFAULT `'skip'` | — |
> 254	| `autopilot_plans.catchup_window` | interval | Yes | DEFAULT `'6 hours'` | — |
> 255	| `cadence_dispatches.idempotency_key` | text | No | UNIQUE **parcial** `WHERE status<>'failed'` | (interno — `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`) |
> 256	| `cadence_dispatches.status` | text | No | transiciona; `sent` só via webhook de status | — |
> 257	| `cadence_dispatches.external_usd_cost` | numeric | Yes | gravado **no reconcile**, nunca no 202 | — |
> 258	| `cadence_dispatches.cost_source` | text | Yes | fonte do custo externo BYOK | — |
> 259	| `social_threads.csw_expires_at` (Fatia 2) | timestamptz | No | derivado do inbound **no servidor** (24h) | — |
> 260	| `social_threads.private_reply_deadline` (Fatia 2) | timestamptz | No | 7d por comentário; `UNIQUE(comment_id)` na fila | — |
> 261	| (fila DM, Fatia 2) `inbound_event_id` | uuid | **No (FK NOT NULL)** | FK → `lead_events(message_received)` do mesmo `user_id` | (cold DM impossível de representar — G1) |
> 262	| `marketing_consents.basis` (WhatsApp/UE) | text | No | gate de enqueue fail-closed; `consent` no Brasil (OTD-CAD-015) | "Consentimento ausente para este canal." / `whatsapp_opt_in_missing` |
> 263	
> 264	**RLS:** `cadence_dispatches` = `select_own` + `insert`/`update` **service-role** (cliente nunca escreve). `autopilot_plans` = RLS owner-scoped preservada. Default-deny em todas.

> 154	        metadata: { plan_id: planId, budget_cap_mco: budgetCap, projected_video: nVideoRunsIfDue * VIDEO_COST, video_every_n_cycles: videoEveryN },
> 155	      }).then(() => {}, () => {});
> 156	    }
> 157	
> 158	    // ── FR-VA-018: trend-informed angle (cycle-level). Reconnects the trends island: pick a trend from vm_trends
> 159	    // (a GLOBAL shared catalog — no tenant scope) to steer THIS cycle's viral angle. Trend text is UNTRUSTED
> 160	    // (Apify/RapidAPI-mined) → the Cyber-Sentinel gates it BEFORE it reaches any prompt (NFR-VA-008), mirroring the
> 161	    // per-product gate in orchestrate-content (skip-and-continue: a blocked trend is dropped, not the whole angle).
> 162	    // Relevance: prefer a trend whose niche overlaps the cycle's products; else the freshest top-viral active trend.
> 163	    // Defense-in-depth: orchestrate-content re-inspects the full topic, so this is the first of two gates.
> 164	    // Graceful: empty vm_trends (the steady state until fetch-trends runs) → angle stays the plain product topic.
> 165	    let viralTopic = plan.name;
> 166	    let trendApplied: string | null = null;
> 167	    try {
> 168	      const { data: prodRows } = await admin
> 169	        .from("vm_affiliate_products").select("niches").in("external_id", products).eq("is_active", true);
> 170	      const productNiches = [...new Set(((prodRows ?? []) as Array<{ niches?: string[] | null }>)
> 171	        .flatMap((p) => p.niches ?? []).filter((n): n is string => typeof n === "string" && n.length > 0))];
> 172	      const fetchTrends = async (filterNiche: boolean) => {
> 173	        let q = admin.from("vm_trends").select("title, niche, viral_score")
> 174	          .eq("is_active", true).order("viral_score", { ascending: false }).limit(5);
> 175	        if (filterNiche && productNiches.length > 0) q = q.in("niche", productNiches);
> 176	        const { data } = await q;
> 177	        return (data ?? []) as Array<{ title: string | null; niche: string | null }>;
> 178	      };
> 179	      let candidates = await fetchTrends(true);
> 180	      if (candidates.length === 0) candidates = await fetchTrends(false);
> 181	      for (const t of candidates) {
> 182	        const clean = sanitizeTrendTitle(String(t.title ?? ""));
> 183	        if (!clean) { console.warn(`[autopilot-run] trend in niche '${t.niche}' rejected by trend gate (unsafe/empty) — skipped`); continue; }
> 184	        // Defense-in-depth: the generic Cyber-Sentinel still runs on the SANITIZED string we will interpolate.
> 185	        const v = inspectPrompt(clean, userId, { rateLimit: false });
> 186	        if (!v.allow) { console.warn(`[autopilot-run] trend in niche '${t.niche}' blocked by sentinel (${v.code}) — skipped`); continue; }
> 187	        trendApplied = clean;
> 188	        // Inert framing: the sanitized keyword is rendered as DATA ("tema em alta"), never as free instruction text.
> 189	        viralTopic = `${plan.name} (tema em alta: ${clean})`;
> 190	        break;
> 191	      }
> 192	    } catch (e) { console.warn(`[autopilot-run] trend angle skipped: ${e}`); }
> 193	
> 194	    // dry_run: return the cost projection (+ resolved trend angle) WITHOUT opening a cycle or deducting (zero-cost).
> 195	    if (dryRun) {
> 196	      return json({ ok: true, dry_run: true, n_runs: nRuns, projected_mco: projected,
> 197	        trend_applied: trendApplied, viral_topic: viralTopic,
> 198	        video: { enabled: videoEnabled, due: videoDue, every_n_cycles: videoEveryN, prior_cycles: priorCycleCount ?? 0, skip_reason: cadence.skipReason, format: videoFormat, cost_each: VIDEO_COST, n_video: nVideoRuns },
> 199	        breakdown: { products: products.length, platforms: platforms.length, ab_variants: abVariants } });
> 200	    }
> 201	
> 202	    // ── begin_autopilot_cycle: ATOMIC cap-check + single pre-debit (or abort reason, no deduct). ────────────────
> 203	    const { data: begun, error: beginErr } = await admin.rpc("begin_autopilot_cycle", {
> 204	      p_plan_id: planId, p_user_id: userId, p_projected_mco: projected, p_planned_units: nRuns,
> 205	    });
> 206	    if (beginErr) { logHealth("error"); return json({ error: "begin_failed", detail: beginErr.message }, 500); }
> 207	    if (!begun?.ok) {
> 208	      const reason = begun?.reason ?? "aborted";
> 209	      // Hard stop (budget too low / insufficient balance) → pause the plan so it stops re-firing every tick.
> 210	      if (reason === "aborted_budget" || reason === "insufficient_balance") {
> 211	        await admin.from("autopilot_plans").update({ is_active: false }).eq("id", planId).eq("user_id", userId);
> 212	      }
> 213	      logHealth("degraded");
> 214	      return json({ ok: false, reason, projected_mco: projected }, reason === "insufficient_balance" ? 402 : 409);
> 215	    }

> 218	    // ── R3 loop (FR-VA-010/011): analyze the PREVIOUS completed cycle to steer THIS cycle's generation. ─────────
> 219	    // collect (sync the live click ledger → creative_metrics) then analyze (→ optimization_policy). Both inline and
> 220	    // PREPAID — the cycle reserved ANALYZE_COST(2); analyzeCharged consumes it only when real data produced a policy
> 221	    // (else finalize refunds the 2). Best-effort — a missing/empty previous cycle just means no steering this round.
> 222	    let analyzeCharged = 0;
> 223	    const svcCall = (fn: string, payload: unknown) => fetch(`${url}/functions/v1/${fn}`, {
> 224	      method: "POST",
> 225	      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${serviceKey}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
> 226	      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
> 227	    });
> 228	    try {

> 231	        .neq("id", cycleId).order("cycle_number", { ascending: false }).limit(1).maybeSingle();
> 232	      if (prev?.id) {
> 233	        await svcCall("autopilot-collect", { cycle_id: prev.id }).catch(() => {});

> 235	          .then((r) => r.json()).catch(() => null);
> 236	        if (ar?.has_real_data) analyzeCharged = ANALYZE_COST;
> 237	      }
> 238	    } catch (e) { console.warn(`[autopilot-run] R3 analyze skipped: ${e}`); }
> 239	
> 240	    // ── Reallocation (FR-VA-030): bias the fan-out toward the policy winner, BUDGET-NEUTRAL (Σ variants stays nRuns
> 241	    // so the pre-debit holds). Winner product×platform gets +1 variant; the last non-winner pair gives up 1 (floor 1
> 242	    // = ε-greedy exploration — never collapse a pair to 0). No-op when ab_variants==1 (no room to move). The policy
> 243	    // is read scoped to THIS plan (mirrors the orchestrate-step fix). This is the lever: the policy now moves the
> 244	    // execution plan, not just the prompt hint.
> 245	    let winProd: string | null = null, winPlat: string | null = null;
> 246	    try {
> 247	      const { data: pol } = await admin.from("optimization_policy")

> 249	        .eq("context->>scope", `viral_autopilot:${planId}`)
> 250	        .order("created_at", { ascending: false }).limit(1).maybeSingle();
> 251	      const ad = (pol as { action_details?: Record<string, unknown> } | null)?.action_details;
> 252	      winProd = (ad?.top_product_id as string) ?? null; winPlat = (ad?.top_platform as string) ?? null;
> 253	    } catch { /* policy read best-effort — never block the cycle */ }
> 254	
> 255	    const pairs: Array<{ product: string; platform: string; n: number }> = [];
> 256	    for (const productId of products) for (const platform of platforms) pairs.push({ product: productId, platform, n: abVariants });
> 257	    if (winProd && winPlat && abVariants >= 2) {
> 258	      const winner = pairs.find((p) => p.product === winProd && p.platform === winPlat);
> 259	      const donor = [...pairs].reverse().find((p) => !(p.product === winProd && p.platform === winPlat) && p.n > 1);
> 260	      if (winner && donor) { winner.n += 1; donor.n -= 1; }   // budget-neutral: Σ n stays = nRuns
> 261	    }
> 262	
> 263	    // ── Fan-out: (product × platform) × reallocated variants → orchestrate-content (prepaid → NO self-bill). ──────
> 264	    let succeeded = 0;
> 265	    let succeededVideo = 0;   // succeeded sub-runs whose flow enqueues a 9:16 render (article-flow / wordpress) — billing reconciliation
> 266	    const tasks: Array<() => Promise<void>> = [];
> 267	    for (const pair of pairs) {
> 268	      for (let v = 0; v < pair.n; v++) {

> 286	            if (r.ok) {

> 290	          } catch (e) {
> 291	            console.warn(`[autopilot-run] sub-run ${pair.product}/${pair.platform} error: ${e}`);
> 292	          }
> 293	        });
> 294	      }
> 295	    }
> 296	    for (let i = 0; i < tasks.length; i += CONCURRENCY) {

> 298	    }

> 300	    // actual = successful runs × 10 + successful ARTICLE-FLOW runs × 12 (only those enqueue a render) + analyzeCharged.
> 301	    // Each succeeded article-flow sub-run enqueued one HyperFrames render (charged_mco marker, no self-bill); a FAILED
> 302	    // render later refunds its 12 via finalize_video_render. The reserved ANALYZE_COST(2) is consumed only when R3
> 303	    // produced a policy from real data; otherwise finalize refunds it. Zero successes → actual = analyzeCharged.
> 304	    const actual = succeeded * ORCH_COST + succeededVideo * VIDEO_COST + analyzeCharged;

> 306	    const { data: fin } = await admin.rpc("finalize_autopilot_cycle", {
> 307	      p_cycle_id: cycleId, p_actual_mco: actual, p_status: status,
> 308	    });

> 311	    if (plan.mode === "every_n_days") {

> 315	    } else {

> 317	    }

> 319	    logHealth("healthy");

The real implementation drains records with `status='queued'` and `scheduled_at`, not the `status='pending'` and `publish_at` that the spec describes. Since the task emphasizes mirroring the living patterns in the repo rather than reinventing, I need to follow what the actual sink is doing. I should trace through how the reshape-pillar component inserts into scheduled_posts to understand the exact shape and column names it's using, since that's the authoritative source for how `metadata.reshape` gets written.

> 107	 * créditos free-tier"). Com uma chave só por chamada, um pool cujo topo está exausto (HTTP 429
> 108	 * `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED`) derruba a geração inteira — que é exatamente o estado REAL da chave do
> 109	 * User 0 hoje. Esta função devolve a fila para o caller tentar a seguinte quando o provedor disser
> 110	 * "sem crédito". Um 429 NÃO enfileira job nem cobra nada: o failover é grátis.
> 111	 *
> 112	 * Ordem = a mesma do `resolveProviderKey` (contrato único): explícita → pool por prioridade → legado.

> 115	export async function listProviderKeyCandidates(
> 116	  admin: AdminClientLike,
> 117	  userId: string,
> 118	  provider: ProviderName,
> 119	  keyId?: string | null,
> 120	): Promise<ResolvedKey[]> {
> 121	  const out: ResolvedKey[] = [];
> 122	  const seen = new Set<string>();
> 123	  const push = (k: ResolvedKey) => {

> 125	    seen.add(k.apiKey);
> 126	    out.push(k);
> 127	  };
> 128	
> 129	  // (1) escolha explícita do usuário — sempre PRIMEIRA (owner-validada).
> 130	  if (keyId && typeof keyId === "string") {

> 132	      .from("decrypted_user_provider_keys")

> 135	      .eq("user_id", userId)

> 140	    }
> 141	  }
> 142	
> 143	  // (2) o pool inteiro, por prioridade crescente.
> 144	  const { data: pool } = await admin
> 145	    .from("decrypted_user_provider_keys")
> 146	    .select("id, api_key, label, metadata")
> 147	    .eq("user_id", userId)
> 148	    .eq("provider", provider)
> 149	    .eq("is_active", true)
> 150	    .order("priority", { ascending: true });
> 151	  for (const row of (pool ?? []) as Array<{ id: string; api_key: string; label: string; metadata: Record<string, unknown> }>) {
> 152	    push({ apiKey: row.api_key, keyId: row.id, source: "pool-priority", label: row.label, metadata: row.metadata, authType: authTypeOf(row.metadata) });
> 153	  }
> 154	
> 155	  // (3) a coluna legada como último recurso.
> 156	  const col = LEGACY_COLUMN[provider];
> 157	  const { data: legacy } = await admin
> 158	    .from("decrypted_user_api_keys")
> 159	    .select(col)
> 160	    .eq("user_id", userId)
> 161	    .maybeSingle();
> 162	  const legacyKey = (legacy as Record<string, unknown> | null)?.[col];
> 163	  if (typeof legacyKey === "string" && legacyKey.length > 0) {

> 165	  }

> 167	  return out;

> 170	/**
> 171	 * Re-resolve EXATAMENTE a chave que já foi usada (o poll de uma operação assíncrona precisa da MESMA
> 172	 * chave do submit — a operação vive no projeto Google dela). Sem isto, um submit que caiu na chave
> 173	 * legada seria pollado com a chave do pool → 404 na operação → estorno de um vídeo que existe.
> 174	 */
> 175	export async function resolveStoredKey(
> 176	  admin: AdminClientLike,
> 177	  userId: string,
> 178	  provider: ProviderName,
> 179	  stored: { keyId?: string | null; source?: string | null },
> 180	): Promise<ResolvedKey | null> {

> 187	  }
> 188	  return candidates[0] ?? null;
> 189	}

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

I'm looking at the exact structure for inserting scheduled posts—it includes the user ID, campaign ID, platform, scheduled timestamp, and a queued status, with metadata containing the content details and channel information. For the cadence-run scenario, I'm working with an existing creative asset rather than a channel variant as the subject.

The challenge is that auto-publish's owner-scoped asset resolution doesn't have a direct path for a bare creative_assets ID—it handles carousel renders via source_job_id, space publish variants through their own table, and channel variants directly, but there's no branch that trusts a raw asset ID from metadata. Since auto-publish won't sign metadata containing asset references (it only trusts certain resolution paths), I need to figure out how to represent the creative asset in the scheduled_posts metadata.reshape structure in a way that auto-publish can actually resolve and sign when the post publishes.

The honest approach: enqueue the cadence dispatch with metadata.reshape.content containing the caption text, and explicitly mark a TODO that owner-scoped asset attachment for a bare creative_assets ID either needs a new auto-publish resolver branch or requires minting an intermediate space_publish_variant or channel_variant row—I won't invent a bucket/key path that the system doesn't already declare. The safest approach is to resolve the creative asset owner-scoped within cadence-run to verify ownership, then enqueue with the caption text in metadata.reshape.content. Since auto-publish only attaches images when there's a channel_variant_id or similar identifier, a bare asset won't trigger image attachment—so I'll add a TODO referencing FR-CAD-006 for future image attachment handling.

But I'm second-guessing whether space_publish_variants could apply here if the creative asset originated from Spaces. The SDD specifies subject = creative_assets.id directly, so I'll stick with resolving the asset owner-scoped for ownership proof and caption, passing the text content, and flagging the image binding gap. The concern is that this makes the dispatch text-only, which feels incomplete—I need to verify whether auto-publish's carousel path works for a single creative asset.

The carousel resolver pulls all images from a source job (minimum two slides), which isn't right for a single asset. So I'm settling on enqueueing the caption via metadata reshape with the content text, and adding a TODO block referencing FR-CAD-006 to handle the signing at publish time when URLs are still valid.

For the auto-publish side, I need to either extend it to resolve the creative_asset_id owner-scoped or create a space_publish_variant — but actually, since the asset attachment seam is genuinely unresolved in the living sink and the SDD flags subject=creative_assets.id, I'll just mark it explicitly as TODO and cite the FR as instructed. That's the right call.

Now moving to FR-CAD-005...

For the MCO charging model: since Fatia 1 uses an existing asset, there's no generate step, so it's 0 MCO. The task says to use deduct_mco_coins atomically, but the SDD only charges via begin/finalize when there's a generate step. I need to reconcile these — the CLAUDE.md specifies atomic RPC charging, so I'm working through how that applies here. The data-model doesn't declare a begin_cadence_cycle RPC, so inventing one would violate the constraint against creating new RPCs—deduct_mco_coins is what exists and what the task specifies. For the budget_cap_mco enforcement, I need to track spending against the cap and halt if a dispatch would exceed the remaining budget, though for Fatia 1 with zero cost this is mostly a safeguard. I'm building a dispatch function that checks this budget constraint.

Now for the gate chain in step 3: I need to validate quiet_hours based on the subject's timezone, check frequency_cap against the channel's cadence profile, enforce the budget check, and handle opt-out logic. The tricky part is opt-out—the SDD describes it for lead-bound scenarios with per-recipient consent, but Fatia 1 publishes to channels without a lead scope, so there's no per-recipient withdrawal to check. I'll only apply the withdrawn_at check if the dispatch is actually lead-bound. So I'm settling on the three core gates from the task spec: ownership resolution first, then quiet_hours based on subject timezone, frequency_cap against the channel cadence, and budget_cap—these are the explicit gates called out, and I'll keep the implementation focused on just these without adding extra features like A/B or digest that would bloat the scope. touch to keep it lightweight. The task's explicit scope covers quiet_hours, frequency_cap, budget_cap_mco, dispatch for scheduled posts, ledger writes, debit operations, and re-arming — I'll implement those precisely along with digest since it shapes the dispatch. A/B testing via FR-CAD-010 needs experiment keys and creative metrics that aren't in scope yet, so I'll document that as a TODO for later.

For frequency_cap, I'm reading the cadence rules from channel_profiles — target_per window (likely day or week), count_min, count_max — then counting prior dispatches for that user and channel within the window to enforce the cap. I need to verify the channel_profiles schema structure first.

For re-arm, I'm computing the next_run_at in UTC from the recurrence pattern (frequency, days, hours, minutes, timezone), which requires converting from the subject's timezone to UTC — that's FR-CAD-007 and needs a generalized calculator.

Before implementing, I should grep through the migrations and schema files to confirm the exact column names in channel_profiles, profiles.timezone, scheduled_posts, and whether cadence_dispatches and plan_kind tables exist yet.

I'm treating `plan.program` as an object with `source_asset_id` and `steps` fields per the sequences contract, then moving on to channel dispatch logic. For each channel in `plan.channel_allowlist`, I'll run the gate chain and dispatch, with digest deduplication ensuring only one post per channel per day.

The digest guard checks if a non-failed dispatch already exists for the same user, channel, and calendar day (in the subject's timezone), referencing FR-CAD-009. For the occurrence itself, I'm setting `occurrence_at` to `plan.next_run_at`, `step_index` to 0 since Fatia 1 has a single step, and building the idempotency key from the plan, step, and occurrence.

But I'm realizing a tension: the migration defines the idempotency key as a hash of just (plan_id, step_index, occurrence_at), which would collapse multiple channels into one dispatch. However, I need to allow one dispatch per channel for the same occurrence. The SDD says the key should be that three-part hash, but that doesn't account for channels. I need to figure out whether the key should include the channel to properly support multi-channel scenarios, or if the step_index itself encodes the channel somehow. Completing the hash with channel included, then setting step_index to the channel ordinal to differentiate dispatches. Now I'm mapping out the gating logic per channel—starting with quiet_hours, which is plan-level, so I compute it once and halt the entire cycle if we're in a quiet window during the subject's timezone.

Next comes the frequency cap gate, which checks the channel's cadence profile to see if we've already hit the max dispatches in the window for this user-channel pair, and halts that specific channel if so. Then the budget cap gate, which tracks cumulative spend across the cycle and blocks dispatch if we'd exceed the budget (though existing assets cost zero, so this is really a guard for future generate steps).

After the gates pass, I insert the scheduled post with the reshaped content and capture its ID, then insert into the cadence ledger with an idempotency key to prevent double-posting if the same dispatch somehow gets retried.

The key insight is ordering: claim the idempotency key first by inserting into the ledger, and only if that succeeds do we enqueue the actual dispatch to the sink. If a concurrent or retried tick tries to claim the same key, it gets nothing back and skips the dispatch entirely—that's the anti-double-post mechanism.

For the debit step, I'm deducting coins before enqueueing, fail-closed so any cost error halts the dispatch and marks it failed. Then I compute the next run time from the recurrence rule and update the automation record.

If the recurrence indicates the program's exhausted, I'll set is_active to false; otherwise for recurring plans like Fatia 1, I always re-arm it. For the observation insertion, I'll make it best-effort with broad error handling so schema mismatches or missing columns don't break the cycle—any failures just get logged to infra. The caption will come from metadata.reshape.content, which contains the text, channel, and surface information.

For the caption text itself, I'll use plan.name as the fallback since that matches how autopilot-run handles topics, though I'll note that richer caption-to-asset bindings are a future improvement. On the coin deduction, I'm using deduct_mco_coins as specified. For the channel allowlist, I need to defensively filter out 'twitter' and 'x' in cadence-run per OTD-CAD-011, even though the plan's allowlist should already exclude them from the cadence-plan level.

For the creative_assets query, I'm keeping it minimal with just `id` and `user_id` columns to resolve ownership, since those are the essential fields needed and source_job_id is already used elsewhere in auto-publish.

Now I'm writing the cadence-run function. The router will pass the user ID via header and plan ID in the body, but cadence-run gates on the Bearer token with SB_SECRET_KEY since it's triggered by cron. I'll load the plan using service-role access without filtering by user, then extract the userId from the plan row itself—that's the authoritative source. For the overlap_policy skip logic, I need to check if there's an existing dispatch with status='publishing' for this plan, which would indicate a prior cycle is still mid-publish.

Actually, I'm reconsidering this. The 'publishing' status is set by auto-publish, not cadence-run itself, so relying on that for overlap detection is fragile. A better approach: if overlap_policy is 'skip' and there's already a pending dispatch for this plan with status in ('queued', 'publishing') and an occurrence_at earlier than the next scheduled run, then skip the new occurrence to prevent stacking. That's simpler and more faithful to the spec.

But I also need to handle the concurrency case—two ticks firing simultaneously. The idempotency key prevents the same occurrence from being dispatched twice, but the real overlap risk is when a prior occurrence is still in-flight. So I'll implement it this way: if policy is 'skip' and there's an in-flight dispatch from a prior occurrence, skip creating the new dispatch but still advance next_run_at. That prevents stacking while ensuring the schedule keeps progressing and doesn't get stuck re-firing the same due instant.

Now I'm setting up the frequency cap window calculation. For a daily window in the subject's timezone, I need to compute the start of that calendar day in UTC. I'll create a helper function that extracts the current date and time components in the given timezone, then use those to calculate the UTC boundary.

For the idempotency key, I'll generate a stable hash using SHA-256 to keep it bounded in the database. I also need to detect whether this is the first successful cycle for a plan by checking if any prior cadence_dispatches records exist — if none do, this run marks the first cycle and should be inserted.

For the dispatch cost, I'm setting it to zero for Fatia 1 since it's an existing asset with no key generation step, but I'll wire up the deduct_mco_coins call path anyway (guarded by cost > 0) so the payment rail is ready even if dormant for now.

Divergence declared inline where it matters.\nimport { createClient } from \"https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2\";\n\nconst corsHeaders = {\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Origin\": \"*\",\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Methods\": \"POST, OPTIONS\",\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Headers\": \"authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type, x-autopilot-user-id\",\n};\nconst UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}$/;\nconst DEFAULT_TZ = \"America/Sao_Paulo\";\n// OTD-CAD-011: X (pay-per-use Post-with-URL = 45 mco) is OUT of Fatia 1. channel_allowlist should already exclude\n// it (enforced at cadence-plan), but we defensively skip+log here so a stale plan can never fan out to X.\nconst FATIA1_EXCLUDED = new Set([\"twitter\", \"x\"]);\n\n// \u2500\u2500 Timezone math (FR-CAD-007 / FM-CAD-06) \u2014 the cron runs in GMT; the subject's wall-clock preference is\n// converted to UTC before it ever touches next_run_at. A self-contained calculator (the separately unit-tested\n// FR-CAD-007 helper is a sibling slice; cadence-run needs a working impl now). \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\ninterface TzParts { y: number; mo: number; d: number; h: number; mi: number; isoDow: number }\nfunction partsInTz(instant: Date, tz: string): TzParts {\n  const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(\"en-US\", {\n    timeZone: tz, year: \"numeric\", month: \"2-digit\", day: \"2-digit\",\n    hour: \"2-digit\", minute: \"2-digit\", second: \"2-digit\", hour12: false, weekday: \"short\",\n  });\n  const p: Record<string, string> = {};\n  for (const part of dtf.formatToParts(instant)) if (part.type !== \"literal\") p[part.type] = part.value;\n  const dowMap: Record<string, number> = { Mon: 1, Tue: 2, Wed: 3, Thu: 4, Fri: 5, Sat: 6, Sun: 7 };\n  return {\n    y: Number(p.year), mo: Number(p.month), d: Number(p.day),\n    h: Number(p.hour === \"24\" ? \"0\" : p.hour), mi: Number(p.minute),\n    isoDow: dowMap[p.weekday] ?? 1,\n  };\n}\n// Convert a wall-clock time in `tz` to the UTC instant (handles DST via the offset at that instant).\nfunction zonedToUtc(y: number, mo: number, d: number, h: number, mi: number, tz: string): Date {\n  const naiveUtc = Date.UTC(y, mo - 1, d, h, mi, 0);\n  // offset(ms) = (what that wall time reads as if it were UTC) - (the same instant rendered in tz)\n  const asIfUtc = partsInTz(new Date(naiveUtc), tz);\n  const rendered = Date.UTC(asIfUtc.y, asIfUtc.mo - 1, asIfUtc.d, asIfUtc.h, asIfUtc.mi, 0);\n  const offset = rendered - naiveUtc;\n  return new Date(naiveUtc - offset);\n}\ninterface Recurrence { frequency?: string; days?: number[]; hours?: number; minutes?: number; tz?: string }\n// Next occurrence strictly AFTER `after`, at hours:minutes in tz, on an allowed day. Returns UTC Date or null.\nfunction computeNextRunAtUtc(rec: Recurrence, tz: string, after: Date): Date | null {\n  const freq = rec.frequency ?? \"daily\";\n  const hours = Math.min(23, Math.max(0, Number(rec.hours ?? 9)));\n  const minutes = Math.min(59, Math.max(0, Number(rec.minutes ?? 0)));\n  const days = Array.isArray(rec.days) ? rec.days.map(Number).filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n)) : [];\n  const startLocal = partsInTz(after, tz);\n  // Walk up to ~14 months of candidate calendar days; the first whose UTC instant > after and whose day matches.\n  for (let i = 0; i < 400; i++) {\n    const cand = new Date(Date.UTC(startLocal.y, startLocal.mo - 1, startLocal.d + i, 12, 0, 0));\n    const cp = partsInTz(cand, tz);\n    let match = false;\n    if (freq === \"daily\") match = true;\n    else if (freq === \"weekly\") match = days.length === 0 || days.includes(cp.isoDow);\n    else if (freq === \"monthly\") match = days.length === 0 || days.includes(cp.d);\n    if (!match) continue;\n    const utc = zonedToUtc(cp.y, cp.mo, cp.d, hours, minutes, tz);\n    if (utc.getTime() > after.getTime()) return utc;\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n// Is `now` inside the plan's quiet window (subject tz)? quiet_hours = {start:'HH:MM', end:'HH:MM', days:int[]}.\nfunction inQuietHours(quiet: unknown, tz: string, now: Date): boolean {\n  const q = (quiet ?? null) as { start?: string; end?: string; days?: number[] } | null;\n  if (!q?.start || !q?.end) return false;\n  const np = partsInTz(now, tz);\n  const qDays = Array.isArray(q.days) ? q.days : [];\n  if (qDays.length > 0 && !qDays.includes(np.isoDow)) return false;\n  const toMin = (s: string) => { const [h, m] = s.split(\":\").map(Number); return (h || 0) * 60 + (m || 0); };\n  const nowMin = np.h * 60 + np.mi;\n  const s = toMin(q.start), e = toMin(q.end);\n  return s <= e ? (nowMin >= s && nowMin < e) : (nowMin >= s || nowMin < e); // wrap past midnight\n}\nasync function sha256Hex(input: string): Promise<string> {\n  const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest(\"SHA-256\", new TextEncoder().encode(input));\n  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf)).map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\")).join(\"\");\n}\n\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\n  if (req.method === \"OPTIONS\") return new Response(\"ok\", { headers: corsHeaders });\n  const json = (b: unknown, s = 200) =>\n    new Response(JSON.stringify(b), { status: s, headers: { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" } });\n\n  const url = Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_URL\")!;\n  const serviceKey = (Deno.env.get(\"SB_SECRET_KEY\") ?? Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\"))!;\n  const admin = createClient(url, serviceKey);\n  // Telemetry chokepoint (NFR-CAD-015). `event`/`metadata` allowlisted \u2014 never leak per-tenant PII into the\n  // global-read infra_health_logs; plan_id/channel/counts only.\n  const pulse = (status: string, event?: string, metadata?: Record<string, unknown>) =>\n    admin.from(\"infra_health_logs\").insert({ service: \"spaces-cadence\", status, event: event ?? null, metadata: metadata ?? {} })\n      .then(() => {}, () => {});\n\n  try {\n    // \u2500\u2500 Identity: cron-only. NEVER a user-facing path (FR-CAD-006 / STRIDE \u00a75.1). \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    if (req.headers.get(\"Authorization\") !== `Bearer ${serviceKey}`) return json({ error: \"Forbidden\" }, 403);\n\n    const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));\n    const planId = typeof body?.plan_id === \"string\" ? body.plan_id : null;\n    if (!planId || !UUID_RE.test(planId)) return json({ error: \"plan_id (uuid) required\" }, 422);\n\n    // \u2500\u2500 (1) DRAIN: load the plan (service-role). user_id is SERVER-TRUSTED from THIS ROW, never the body. \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    const { data: plan } = await admin\n      .from(\"autopilot_plans\")\n      .select(\"id, user_id, project_id, name, plan_kind, is_active, next_run_at, recurrence, quiet_hours, overlap_policy, program, channel_allowlist, budget_cap_mco\")\n      .eq(\"id\", planId).maybeSingle();\n    if (!plan) return json({ error: \"plan_not_found\" }, 404);\n    if (plan.plan_kind !== \"cadence\") return json({ error: \"not_a_cadence_plan\", plan_kind: plan.plan_kind }, 409);\n    if (plan.is_active !== true) return json({ ok: false, reason: \"inactive\" }, 200);\n\n    const userId = plan.user_id as string;                 // \u2190 the only trusted tenant identity (from the row)\n    const rec = (plan.recurrence ?? {}) as Recurrence;\n    const tz = (typeof rec.tz === \"string\" && rec.tz) ? rec.tz : await resolveSubjectTz(admin, userId);\n    const occurrenceAt = plan.next_run_at ? new Date(plan.next_run_at as string) : new Date();\n    const overlapPolicy = (plan.overlap_policy as string) ?? \"skip\";\n\n    // \u2500\u2500 (2) RESOLVE SUBJECT owner-scoped (Fatia 1 = an existing creative_asset \u21d2 0 mco, keyless \u2014 OTD-CAD-002=A).\n    // The subject asset id is carried by the plan's `program` jsonb (the CadenceData.sourceAssetId persisted by\n    // cadence-plan / FR-CAD-005). Tolerant read: {source_asset_id} on the program object, or on step 0 of an array.\n    // No source_asset_id COLUMN exists (migration 20260716120000 added none) \u2014 so `program` is the contract seam.\n    const program = plan.program as Record<string, unknown> | Array<Record<string, unknown>> | null;\n    const sourceAssetId = extractSourceAssetId(program);\n    if (!sourceAssetId) {\n      await pulse(\"degraded\", \"cadence_no_subject\", { plan_id: planId });\n      // Re-arm anyway so a mis-armed plan does not re-fire the same due instant every tick.\n      await reArm(admin, planId, userId, rec, tz, occurrenceAt);\n      // TODO(FR-CAD-005): cadence-plan MUST persist sourceAssetId into program ({source_asset_id, steps}).\n      return json({ ok: false, reason: \"no_subject\" }, 200);\n    }\n    const { data: asset } = await admin\n      .from(\"creative_assets\").select(\"id, user_id\")\n      .eq(\"id\", sourceAssetId).eq(\"user_id\", userId).maybeSingle();   // hard tenant bind \u2014 a forged id \u2192 0 rows\n    if (!asset) {\n      await pulse(\"degraded\", \"cadence_subject_not_owned\", { plan_id: planId });\n      await reArm(admin, planId, userId, rec, tz, occurrenceAt);\n      return json({ ok: false, reason: \"subject_not_owned\" }, 200);\n    }\n\n    // \u2500\u2500 Overlap policy (FM-CAD-01): a prior-occurrence dispatch still in flight \u21d2 skip THIS occurrence (never\n    // stack), but still re-arm so the plan progresses. Idempotency (below) covers concurrent same-occurrence ticks.\n    if (overlapPolicy === \"skip\") {\n      const { count: inflight } = await admin\n        .from(\"cadence_dispatches\").select(\"id\", { count: \"exact\", head: true })\n        .eq(\"plan_id\", planId).eq(\"user_id\", userId)\n        .in(\"status\", [\"queued\", \"publishing\"])\n        .lt(\"occurrence_at\", occurrenceAt.toISOString());\n      if ((inflight ?? 0) > 0) {\n        await pulse(\"degraded\", \"cadence_overlap_skip\", { plan_id: planId, inflight });\n        await reArm(admin, planId, userId, rec, tz, occurrenceAt);\n        return json({ ok: true, reason: \"overlap_skip\", dispatched: 0 }, 200);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // \u2500\u2500 (3) GATE CHAIN \u2014 server-side, NEVER an LLM. \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    const now = new Date();\n    // Gate A \u2014 quiet_hours in the subject's timezone \u21d2 HALT the whole cycle (suppress, do not reenqueue).\n    if (inQuietHours(plan.quiet_hours, tz, now)) {\n      await pulse(\"degraded\", \"cadence_halt_quiet_hours\", { plan_id: planId });\n      await reArm(admin, planId, userId, rec, tz, occurrenceAt);\n      return json({ ok: true, reason: \"quiet_hours\", dispatched: 0 }, 200);\n    }\n\n    // Channels for this cycle (per-plan allowlist MINUS X \u2014 OTD-CAD-011).\n    const rawChannels = (Array.isArray(plan.channel_allowlist) ? plan.channel_allowlist : [])\n      .filter((c): c is string => typeof c === \"string\" && c.length > 0);\n    const channels = rawChannels.filter((c) => {\n      if (FATIA1_EXCLUDED.has(c)) { void pulse(\"degraded\", \"cadence_channel_excluded_fatia1\", { plan_id: planId, channel: c }); return false; }\n      return true;\n    });\n    if (channels.length === 0) {\n      await reArm(admin, planId, userId, rec, tz, occurrenceAt);\n      return json({ ok: false, reason: \"no_channels\" }, 200);\n    }\n\n    // Gate C setup \u2014 budget_cap_mco (in mcoCoins, never USD). Fatia 1 dispatch of an EXISTING asset costs 0 (keyless,\n    // OTD-CAD-002=A); the debit rail below stays wired (deduct_mco_coins) for a future `generate` step. G7 remains a\n    // CYCLE invariant, not a per-node one. budget_cap_mco is the ceiling for that (future) spend.\n    const budgetCap = Number(plan.budget_cap_mco ?? 0);\n    let spentThisCycle = 0;\n\n    // First-ever dispatch for this plan? (Mesh Connection Mandate \u2014 observation node on the first successful cycle.)\n    const { count: priorDispatches } = await admin\n      .from(\"cadence_dispatches\").select(\"id\", { count: \"exact\", head: true })\n      .eq(\"plan_id\", planId).eq(\"user_id\", userId);\n    const isFirstCycle = (priorDispatches ?? 0) === 0;\n\n    const results: Array<{ channel: string; status: string; dispatch_id?: string; scheduled_post_id?: string }> = [];\n    let dispatched = 0;\n\n    for (let idx = 0; idx < channels.length; idx++) {\n      const channel = channels[idx];\n      try {\n        // Gate B \u2014 frequency_cap vs channel_profiles.cadence (authoritative ceiling \u2014 OTD-CAD-008). Scope\n        // (user_id, channel) on Fatia 1 (OTD-CAD-017=A). HALT (no reenqueue \u2014 Knock semantics).\n        const cap = await frequencyCap(admin, userId, channel);\n        if (cap) {\n          const windowStart = capWindowStart(cap.target_per, tz, now);\n          const { count: usedInWindow } = await admin\n            .from(\"cadence_dispatches\").select(\"id\", { count: \"exact\", head: true })\n            .eq(\"user_id\", userId).eq(\"channel\", channel)\n            .neq(\"status\", \"failed\")\n            .gte(\"occurrence_at\", windowStart.toISOString());\n          if ((usedInWindow ?? 0) >= cap.count_max) {\n            await pulse(\"degraded\", \"cadence_halt_freq_cap\", { plan_id: planId, channel, cap: cap.count_max, target_per: cap.target_per });\n            results.push({ channel, status: \"halt_freq_cap\" });\n            continue;\n          }\n        }\n\n        // Gate \u2014 dedup/digest (user, channel, day): collapse N same-day due instants into 1 publication (FR-CAD-009).\n        const dayStart = capWindowStart(\"day\", tz, occurrenceAt);\n        const { count: sameDay } = await admin\n          .from(\"cadence_dispatches\").select(\"id\", { count: \"exact\", head: true })\n          .eq(\"user_id\", userId).eq(\"channel\", channel)\n          .neq(\"status\", \"failed\")\n          .gte(\"occurrence_at\", dayStart.toISOString());\n        if ((sameDay ?? 0) > 0) {\n          results.push({ channel, status: \"digest_collapsed\" });\n          continue;\n        }\n\n        // Cost of THIS dispatch. Fatia 1 = existing asset \u21d2 0 (keyless). A future `generate` step would set this > 0.\n        const dispatchCostMco = 0;\n        // Gate C \u2014 budget_cap_mco. Only meaningful once dispatchCostMco > 0 (future generate step).\n        if (dispatchCostMco > 0 && budgetCap > 0 && spentThisCycle + dispatchCostMco > budgetCap) {\n          await pulse(\"degraded\", \"cadence_halt_budget_cap\", { plan_id: planId, channel, budget_cap_mco: budgetCap });\n          results.push({ channel, status: \"halt_budget_cap\" });\n          continue;\n        }\n\n        // \u2500\u2500 (5\u21924 ordering) LEDGER-FIRST claim of the idempotency key, THEN enqueue the sink. Claiming BEFORE the\n        // scheduled_posts INSERT is what makes a concurrent/retry tick a NO-OP instead of a double-post: whoever\n        // loses the ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING never reaches the enqueue. (\u00a75.2 / FM-CAD-01.)\n        const stepIndex = idx;                              // one row per (occurrence, channel); step encodes channel ordinal\n        const idempotencyKey = await sha256Hex(`${planId}:${stepIndex}:${occurrenceAt.toISOString()}:${channel}`);\n        const { data: claim } = await admin\n          .from(\"cadence_dispatches\")\n          .insert({\n            user_id: userId, plan_id: planId, step_index: stepIndex, occurrence_at: occurrenceAt.toISOString(),\n            idempotency_key: idempotencyKey, channel, status: \"queued\",\n          })\n          .onConflict(\"idempotency_key\").ignoreDuplicates(true)\n          .select(\"id\").maybeSingle();\n        if (!claim?.id) {\n          // Conflict \u21d2 this (plan, occurrence, channel) was already dispatched by a prior/concurrent tick. NO-OP.\n          results.push({ channel, status: \"already_dispatched\" });\n          continue;\n        }\n        const dispatchId = claim.id as string;\n\n        // \u2500\u2500 Atomic debit BEFORE the send, fail-closed (NFR-CAD-007 \u2014 cobran\u00e7a s\u00f3 via RPC at\u00f4mica, nunca client-side).\n        // deduct_mco_coins(p_user_id, p_amount) raises on insufficient balance. Dormant at Fatia 1 (cost 0).\n        if (dispatchCostMco > 0) {\n          const { error: dErr } = await admin.rpc(\"deduct_mco_coins\", { p_user_id: userId, p_amount: dispatchCostMco });\n          if (dErr) {\n            // Debit failed \u21d2 release the idempotency key (status='failed') so the next tick may retry this occurrence.\n            await admin.from(\"cadence_dispatches\").update({ status: \"failed\", error: \"insufficient_balance\" }).eq(\"id\", dispatchId);\n            await pulse(\"degraded\", \"cadence_debit_failed\", { plan_id: planId, channel, amount: dispatchCostMco });\n            results.push({ channel, status: \"debit_failed\", dispatch_id: dispatchId });\n            continue;\n          }\n          spentThisCycle += dispatchCostMco;\n        }\n\n        // \u2500\u2500 (4) DISPATCH: enqueue into the LIVING scheduled_posts sink (mirrors reshape-pillar:487 exactly).\n        // auto-publish drains WHERE status='queued' AND scheduled_at<=now() (NOT publish_at/pending \u2014 that is the\n        // sealed \u00a77 nomenclature; the applied sink uses scheduled_at/queued). metadata.reshape carries the caption;\n        // the asset image ref is NEVER placed in metadata (FMEA-011 \u2014 auto-publish resolves refs owner-scoped).\n        //\n        // TODO(FR-CAD-006 \u00b7 auto-publish): a bare creative_assets.id has NO owner-scoped resolver branch in\n        // auto-publish (it resolves channel_variant_id / space_publish_variant_id / carousel_render_id). Binding the\n        // subject's IMAGE to the post requires either (a) an auto-publish branch that signs a creative_assets.id\n        // owner-scoped, or (b) minting a space_publish_variant row. Until then this enqueues the CAPTION only.\n        const content: Record<string, unknown> = { text: (plan.name as string) ?? \"\", channel };\n        const { data: sp, error: spErr } = await admin\n          .from(\"scheduled_posts\")\n          .insert({\n            user_id: userId, content_id: null, campaign_id: null, social_account_id: null,\n            platform: channel, scheduled_at: new Date().toISOString(), status: \"queued\",\n            metadata: { reshape: { content, source_asset_id: sourceAssetId, channel }, cadence: { plan_id: planId, dispatch_id: dispatchId } },\n          })\n          .select(\"id\").single();\n        if (spErr || !sp?.id) {\n          // Enqueue failed \u21d2 release the key (retry next tick). If we debited, the failure is surfaced (a refund of a\n          // generate step is deferred \u2014 Fatia 1 cost is 0, so no mint-hole here).\n          await admin.from(\"cadence_dispatches\").update({ status: \"failed\", error: spErr?.message ?? \"enqueue_failed\" }).eq(\"id\", dispatchId);\n          await pulse(\"error\", \"cadence_enqueue_failed\", { plan_id: planId, channel });\n          results.push({ channel, status: \"enqueue_failed\", dispatch_id: dispatchId });\n          continue;\n        }\n        // Link the ledger row to the sink. status stays 'queued' \u2014 it becomes 'sent' ONLY on the status webhook\n        // (PROC-CAD-05 / FM-CAD-05), never from a 202. external_usd_cost/cost_source are written on that reconcile.\n        await admin.from(\"cadence_dispatches\").update({ target_ref: sp.id }).eq(\"id\", dispatchId);\n        await pulse(\"healthy\", \"cadence_dispatch\", { plan_id: planId, channel });\n        results.push({ channel, status: \"dispatched\", dispatch_id: dispatchId, scheduled_post_id: sp.id });\n        dispatched++;\n      } catch (chErr) {\n        await pulse(\"error\", \"cadence_channel_error\", { plan_id: planId, channel });\n        console.warn(`[cadence-run] channel ${channel} error:`, chErr instanceof Error ? chErr.message : chErr);\n        results.push({ channel, status: \"error\" });\n      }\n    }\n\n    // \u2500\u2500 (6) RE-ARM next_run_at in UTC (FR-CAD-007) \u2014 or deactivate if no future occurrence exists. \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n    const armed = await reArm(admin, planId, userId, rec, tz, occurrenceAt);\n\n    // \u2500\u2500 Mesh Connection Mandate: observation node on the FIRST successful cycle of the plan (best-effort \u2014 a mesh\n    // hiccup NEVER blocks the money/dispatch path; failure is logged to infra_health_logs).\n    if (isFirstCycle && dispatched > 0) {\n      try {\n        const { data: node } = await admin.from(\"mcorch_nodes\").insert({\n          user_id: userId, project_id: plan.project_id ?? null, node_type: \"observation\",\n          title: `Cadence cycle \u00b7 plan ${planId} \u00b7 ${channels.join(\",\")}`,\n          content: `Cadence ${plan.name ?? \"\"} dispatched ${dispatched} channel(s): ${channels.join(\", \")}. budget_cap_mco=${budgetCap}.`,\n        }).select(\"id\").maybeSingle();\n        if (node?.id) {\n          await fetch(`${url}/functions/v1/embed-mcorch-node`, {\n            method: \"POST\",\n            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${serviceKey}`, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n            body: JSON.stringify({ node_id: node.id, content: `Cadence ${plan.name ?? \"\"} ${channels.join(\" \")}` }),\n          }).catch(() => {});\n        }\n      } catch (e) {\n        await pulse(\"degraded\", \"cadence_mesh_observation_failed\", { plan_id: planId });\n        console.warn(\"[cadence-run] mesh observation skipped:\", e instanceof Error ? e.message : e);\n      }\n    }\n\n    return json({ ok: true, plan_id: planId, dispatched, next_run_at: armed, results });\n  } catch (e) {\n    await pulse(\"error\", \"cadence_fatal\");\n    return json({ error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : \"unknown\" }, 500);\n  }\n});\n\n// \u2500\u2500 helpers \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n\n// Subject timezone cascade (FR-CAD-007): recurrence.tz (resolved by caller) \u2192 profiles.timezone \u2192 default.\n// profiles.timezone may not exist yet (no migration adds it) \u2014 read defensively; a missing column degrades to\n// the default rather than throwing. TODO(FR-CAD-007): add profiles.timezone (dead-data first-reader per SDD \u00a72.5).\n// deno-lint-ignore no-explicit-any\nasync function resolveSubjectTz(admin: any, userId: string): Promise<string> {\n  try {\n    const { data } = await admin.from(\"profiles\").select(\"timezone\").eq(\"id\", userId).maybeSingle();\n    const tz = (data as { timezone?: string } | null)?.timezone;\n    if (typeof tz === \"string\" && tz.length > 0) return tz;\n  } catch { /* column absent / read error \u2192 default */ }\n  return DEFAULT_TZ;\n}\n\n// The subject asset id lives in the plan's `program` jsonb (the seam cadence-plan writes \u2014 FR-CAD-005).\nfunction extractSourceAssetId(program: unknown): string | null {\n  if (!program) return null;\n  const pick = (o: Record<string, unknown> | undefined) => {\n    const v = o?.source_asset_id ?? o?.sourceAssetId;\n    return typeof v === \"string\" && UUID_RE.test(v) ? v : null;\n  };\n  if (Array.isArray(program)) return pick(program[0] as Record<string, unknown>);\n  return pick(program as Record<string, unknown>);\n}\n\n// channel_profiles.cadence = {target_per: day|week|month, count_min, count_max, note}. Authoritative per-channel\n// ceiling (OTD-CAD-008). Prefer the tenant's own row; fall back to a system row (user_id IS NULL). Multiple\n// surfaces per channel \u2192 take the most permissive count_max (channel-level ceiling). Absent \u21d2 no cap (allow).\n// deno-lint-ignore no-explicit-any\nasync function frequencyCap(admin: any, userId: string, channel: string): Promise<{ count_max: number; target_per: string } | null> {\n  const read = async (scopeOwn: boolean) => {\n    let q = admin.from(\"channel_profiles\").select(\"cadence\").eq(\"channel\", channel);\n    q = scopeOwn ? q.eq(\"user_id\", userId) : q.is(\"user_id\", null);\n    const { data } = await q;\n    return (data ?? []) as Array<{ cadence: { target_per?: string; count_max?: number } | null }>;\n  };\n  let rows = await read(true);\n  if (rows.length === 0) rows = await read(false);\n  let best: { count_max: number; target_per: string } | null = null;\n  for (const r of rows) {\n    const c = r.cadence;\n    if (!c || typeof c.count_max !== \"number\") continue;\n    if (!best || c.count_max > best.count_max) best = { count_max: c.count_max, target_per: c.target_per ?? \"day\" };\n  }\n  return best;\n}\n\n// Window start for a frequency-cap window, anchored to the subject timezone. 'day' \u2192 start of the local day;\n// 'week'/'month' \u2192 rolling 7d/30d (pragmatic; a calendar-week/month refinement is a later slice).\nfunction capWindowStart(targetPer: string, tz: string, ref: Date): Date {\n  if (targetPer === \"week\") return new Date(ref.getTime() - 7 * 86_400_000);\n  if (targetPer === \"month\") return new Date(ref.getTime() - 30 * 86_400_000);\n  const p = partsInTz(ref, tz);\n  return zonedToUtc(p.y, p.mo, p.d, 0, 0, tz); // start of the subject-local day\n}\n\n// Re-arm next_run_at in UTC, or deactivate the plan when no future occurrence exists. Returns the ISO next_run_at\n// (or null when deactivated). NEVER lets the cron carry a wall-clock preference \u2014 the subject time is converted.\n// deno-lint-ignore no-explicit-any\nasync function reArm(admin: any, planId: string, userId: string, rec: Recurrence, tz: string, after: Date): Promise<string | null> {\n  const next = computeNextRunAtUtc(rec, tz, after);\n  if (next) {\n    await admin.from(\"autopilot_plans\").update({ next_run_at: next.toISOString() }).eq(\"id\", planId).eq(\"user_id\", userId);\n    return next.toISOString();\n  }\n  await admin.from(\"autopilot_plans\").update({ is_active: false }).eq(\"id\", planId).eq(\"user_id\", userId);\n  return null;\n}\n"
```json
}
```
